## Summary
<p>This is a gateway-only <code>extended-stable</code> release, which is our current equivalent to LTS. This release is OpenClaw from the end of September 2026, plus critical security updates, reliability and performance fixes, and features like new model support. The latest version of OpenClaw at the time of this release is <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/releases#release-v2026.10.3">2026.10.3</a></p>
<h2>2026.9.33</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Safer September upgrades:</strong> updates on the 2026.9 line keep state, plugin data, service ownership, and Windows state paths intact, and refuse automatic restore over unattributed Doctor writes. <h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Update and Doctor:</strong> keep Doctor running under unreadable supervisors and child binding errors, keep clean service install refusals out of native-authority recovery, stop update history writes from stalling cron and cleanup, avoid bookkeeping-only repair refusals, and never delete an OAuth profile because the legacy auth store is unreadable. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5809279856" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169665" <li><strong>Updating from 2026.8.35:</strong> cron jobs approved with "allow always" keep running unattended after the update, pnpm 12 Gateway services left on a deleted store generation are rewritten to the durable install link, Doctor defers optional checks so updates finish under older updaters' time limits, official plugins with removed SDK imports are no longer silently kept, and Kubernetes pods run Doctor before starting. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5842745939" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171816" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171816/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171816">#171816</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Gateway responsiveness:</strong> long streamed replies that start with a reply tag such as <code>[[reply_to_current]]</code> no longer re-parse the whole reply on every delta, which could keep the Gateway busy for tens of seconds. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5752892713" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166781" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166781/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166781">#166781</a> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5752896288" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166782" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166782/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166782">#166782</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/boeingchoco/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/boeingchoco">@boeingchoco</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Tailscale:</strong> an interrupted Tailscale Serve start stops its serve process, the Gateway keeps local access when the tailnet denies Serve, and public chat no longer returns 503s right after startup. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5768232507" data-permission-text="Title is private" ## Why It Matters
Source signal: <p>This is a gateway-only <code>extended-stable</code> release, which is our current equivalent to LTS. This release is OpenClaw from the end of September 2026, plus critical security updates, reliability and performance fixes, and features like new model support. The latest version of OpenClaw at the time of this release is <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/releases#release-v2026.10.3">2026.10.3</a></p>
<h2>2026.9.33</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Safer September upgrades:</strong> updates on the 2026.9 line keep state, plugin data, service ownership, and Windows state paths intact, and refuse automatic restore over unattributed Doctor writes. <h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Update and Doctor:</strong> keep Doctor running under unreadable supervisors and child binding errors, keep clean service install refusals out of native-authority recovery, stop update history writes from stalling cron and cleanup, avoid bookkeeping-only repair refusals, and never delete an OAuth profile because the legacy auth store is unreadable. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5809279856" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/169665" <li><strong>Updating from 2026.8.35:</strong> cron jobs approved with "allow always" keep running unattended after the update, pnpm 12 Gateway services left on a deleted store generation are rewritten to the durable install link, Doctor defers optional checks so updates finish under older updaters' time limits, official plugins with removed SDK imports are no longer silently kept, and Kubernetes pods run Doctor before starting. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5842745939" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/171816" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171816/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171816">#171816</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Gateway responsiveness:</strong> long streamed replies that start with a reply tag such as <code>[[reply_to_current]]</code> no longer re-parse the whole reply on every delta, which could keep the Gateway busy for tens of seconds. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5752892713" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166781" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166781/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166781">#166781</a> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5752896288" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/166782" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166782/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166782">#166782</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/boeingchoco/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/boeingchoco">@boeingchoco</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Tailscale:</strong> an interrupted Tailscale Serve start stops its serve process, the Gateway keeps local access when the tailnet denies Serve, and public chat no longer returns 503s right after startup. <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5768232507" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167436" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167436/hovercard" <li><strong>Context engine:</strong> sessions holding an old blocked turn no longer repeat the blocked-turn warning and re-read it on every run. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5772486285" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/167595" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167595/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167595">#167595</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/jalehman/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/jalehman">@jalehman</a>.</li>
<li><strong>State database:</strong> state reads no longer stay closed until a Gateway restart after a failed state cleanup. Fixes <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5652044933" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162085" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162085/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/162085">#162085</a>.
